### test_index_highcardinality_stress.cpp

#### `TEST(IndexHighCardinalityStress, ConcurrentIndexReadWrite)`
- Source: `tests/index/test_index_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:197
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IndexHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (ConcurrentIndexReadWrite): n/a
- Details: TestConcurrentIndexReadWrite Runs 4 writer threads (each inserting 10 000 vectors) concurrently with 4 reader threads (each issuing 10 000 top-5 queries) against a shared stub store. Asserts no read/write errors. Log pattern: [INDEX:HNSWCorruption]

#### `TEST(IndexHighCardinalityStress, HighCardinalityIndexBuild)`
- Source: `tests/index/test_index_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:149
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IndexHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (HighCardinalityIndexBuild): n/a
- Details: TestHighCardinalityIndexBuild Inserts 1 000 000 vectors into the stub store across 8 threads and asserts that the final size equals exactly 1 000 000. Log pattern: [INDEX:RebuildStall]

#### `TEST(IndexHighCardinalityStress, MultiBackendStress)`
- Source: `tests/index/test_index_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:270
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IndexHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (MultiBackendStress): n/a
- Details: TestMultiBackendStress Inserts 10 000 vectors into each of three stub backends (FLAT, HNSW, IVFPQ) using dedicated writer threads and issues 5 000 queries against each backend from dedicated reader threads. Asserts all backends reach the expected size and return consistent results. Log pattern: [INDEX:GPUKernelFallback]

#### `Status addEdge(const BaseEntity &edge)`
- Source: `include/index/graph_index.h`:103
- Brief: Fügt eine Kante zum Graphen hinzu (atomare Operation über WriteBatch).
- Parameters:
  - `edge` (const BaseEntity &): Input parameter.
- Return: Status::OK() bei erfolgreicher Einfügung, Status::Error() bei Validierungsfehlern (fehlende Felder, leere Node-IDs).
- Details: Add Edge. Edge-Operationen (Edge-Entity benötigt Felder: id, _from, _to) edge BaseEntity mit erforderlichen Feldern: id: Eindeutige Kanten-ID (non-empty) _from: Quell-Knoten-ID (non-empty, fail-closed QW-45 Guard) _to: Ziel-Knoten-ID (non-empty, fail-closed QW-45 Guard) Status::OK() bei erfolgreicher Einfügung, Status::Error() bei Validierungsfehlern (fehlende Felder, leere Node-IDs). QW-45 Fail-Closed Guard: Empty or missing _from/_to node IDs are rejected before persistence to prevent graph topology corruption. The guards ensure that every edge has valid, non-empty source and target node references. Any validation failure returns Status::Error() immediately (fail-closed behavior). edge Input parameter. Return value. Calls: isOpen(), Status::Error(), getFieldAsString(), empty(), THEMIS_ERROR(), createWriteBatch(), rollback(), commit().

- Source: `include/index/graph_index.h`:115
- Brief: Add Edge.
- Parameters:
  - `edge` (const BaseEntity &): Input parameter.
  - `txn` (RocksDBWrapper::TransactionWrapper &): Input/output parameter.
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Transaction variant for MVCC isolation. Applies same QW-45 fail-closed guards as the main addEdge method. edge Input parameter. txn Input/output parameter. Return value. Calls: isActive(), Status::Error(), getFieldAsString(), empty(), THEMIS_ERROR(), nlohmann::json::parse(), is_array(), is_string().

#### `void setAqlQueryExecutor(AqlQueryExecutorFn fn)`
- Source: `include/index/process_graph.h`:719
- Brief: Inject an AQL query executor for index-backed multi-model queries.
- Parameters:
  - `fn` (AqlQueryExecutorFn): Input parameter.
- Details: ============================================================================ PERMANENT FALLBACK NOTE (ProcessGraph Multi-Model Query — in-process RocksDB scan): Purpose: Implement AQL-style multi-model queries (form-data filter, foreign-key join, aggregation) directly over the RocksDB in-process store using scanPrefix(). When a non-null executor is provided the three multi-model query methods will build an AQL statement, invoke the executor, and map the results back to their typed return values. The in-process O(n) scan fallbacks are retained and used when no executor is set. Thread safety: call before any concurrent query; the function object is read under a shared lock from query methods. fn Input parameter. This avoids requiring a live ArangoDB/ThemisDB query engine for process graph analytics. The functions provide real query semantics for the in-process database. Activation: Always active — these functions run against the in-process RocksDB store regardless of whether a remote query engine is configured. Production Delta: Filter and join operations use O(n) full scans instead of index-backed AQL traversals. Performance degrades with large process instances (> 10 K tokens per process): queryTasksByFormData is O(n) in tokens, queryForeignKeyJoin is O(n×m) in tokens×foreign docs. In production with a live query engine these would be replaced by AQL queries with server-side index acceleration. Note: Wire a ThemisDB AQL query engine reference via setAqlQueryExecutor() to delegate to engine-backed queries; the in-process scan path then becomes the offline fallback. Roadmap ref: src/index/FUTURE_ENHANCEMENTS.md §"Process Graph Multi-Model Query Engine" ============================================================================ Multi-Model Query Implementation ============================================================================ Calls: std::move().

#### `void setEmbedder(std::function< std::vector< float >(std::string_view)> embedder)`
- Source: `include/index/process_graph.h`:527
- Brief: Wire a text-embedding function for auto-generating process embeddings.
- Parameters:
  - `embedder` (std::function< std::vector< float >(std::string_view)>): (std::string_view text) → std::vector<float>. Pass an empty function to disable.
- Details: When set, registerProcess() automatically computes and persists an embedding for the process name (and BPMN description if available) so that findSimilarProcesses() / semanticSearchProcesses() work without manual pre-computation. embedder (std::string_view text) → std::vector<float>. Pass an empty function to disable.

#### `std::pair< Status, IncrementalReindexStats > incrementalReindex(float rebuild_threshold=0.20f, std::string_view vectorField="embedding")`
- Source: `include/index/vector_index.h`:205
- Brief: Incrementally re-indexes the HNSW index by syncing with current storage state without performing a full rebuild.
- Parameters:
  - `rebuild_threshold` (float): Deleted-label fraction that triggers full rebuild (0.0–1.0). Pass 0 to disable automatic full rebuild.
  - `vectorField` (std::string_view): Name of the vector field in entities (default "embedding").
- Return: {Status, IncrementalReindexStats}
- Details: Compares in-memory index state against storage and: Adds new vectors found in storage but missing from index Marks deleted vectors present in index but removed from storage Updates vectors whose data has changed in storage Skips unchanged vectors (preserves HNSW graph connectivity) If the ratio of soft-deleted HNSW labels exceeds rebuild_threshold, a full rebuild is triggered automatically and IncrementalReindexStats::full_rebuild_triggered is set to true. rebuild_threshold Deleted-label fraction that triggers full rebuild (0.0–1.0). Pass 0 to disable automatic full rebuild. vectorField Name of the vector field in entities (default "embedding"). {Status, IncrementalReindexStats}

- Details: Truncate vector (raw array of length full_dim) to a vector of length trunc_dim_. If full_dim >= trunc_dim_, only the first trunc_dim_ elements are kept. If full_dim < trunc_dim_, all available elements are copied and the remaining positions are zero-padded up to trunc_dim_, regardless of normalize_.

#### `Status bulkLoad(std::string_view table, const std::vector< std::pair< std::string, geo::GeoSidecar > > &entries)`
- Source: `include/index/spatial_index.h`:170
- Brief: Bulk-load a collection of (primary_key, sidecar) pairs into the spatial index for a given table.
- Parameters:
  - `table` (std::string_view): Name of the table whose spatial index should be populated.
  - `entries` (const std::vector< std::pair< std::string, geo::GeoSidecar > > &): Vector of {primary_key, GeoSidecar} pairs to index.
- Return: Status::OK() on success or Status::Error() if the table has no spatial index registered.
- Details: Uses STR (Sort-Tile-Recursive) packing via GeoRTree::bulkLoad(), which is 3–5× faster than incremental insertion for read-heavy workloads. Replaces any previously cached in-memory R-tree for the table. Memory usage is reported as a structured log entry with field geo_index_bytes_allocated after the bulk load completes. table Name of the table whose spatial index should be populated. entries Vector of {primary_key, GeoSidecar} pairs to index. Status::OK() on success or Status::Error() if the table has no spatial index registered.
